# Standing approval oo-9n5p9 (delegated by Jon 2026-10-05; record docs/decisions/delegated-2026-10-05.md).
# Scope: a façade-deletion bead (label sweep:objc-bridge, "Delete X+ObjCBridge") may retire ONLY the
# test cases and stand-ins that exercise the deleted façade - its selectors, oo::ToObjC/oo::ToCxx
# crossings, the façade's -dealloc. The class's C++ API checks stay and must still pass; nothing else
# is removed or weakened under it. This block approves no path by itself: each deletion bead lists
# the cases it retires and gets its own path line(s) here, reason starting "oo-9n5p9:", landed by a
# change before its deletion (a change that deletes a listed test may not also edit this file).
